No picture puzzles. Decided in the Star testing session; **the path is shared with Color Belt's form, so this applies to it too unless changed there** (flagged in the Star status file for the Color Belt session). Rejected: invisible checks only; a picture puzzle (reCAPTCHA; accessibility and privacy). |

| Star testing Candidates: removing a candidate (decided v0.113) | (See v0.508: the Removed group is collapsed by default; admins get Delete permanently on withdrawn rows; buttons "Keep candidate" / "Delete permanently".) Option C (Jeffrey). Admins only (v0.110); removing the extra copy of a "Possible duplicate" is a separate staff action (v0.110). A candidate's ⋯ menu offers two actions. (1) **Withdraw** (`ti-user-minus`, hint "Kept under Removed; can be restored"). It happens at once with a 5-second Undo toast (rule 1.4). The row moves to a **Removed** group at the bottom of that Dan's list, with a gray "Removed" tag, "removed <date> by <name>" and any recorded payment still shown. Withdrawn candidates are left out of Grading, the Dan counts and results. **Restore** in the removed row's ⋯ brings the candidate back. (2) **Delete permanently** (red, `ti-trash`). A confirmation names exactly what goes, e.g. "Remove Mia Patel? Her registration and the $140 cash payment recorded on Oct 15 are deleted from Fall 2026 Star Testing. This can't be undone." The buttons are Keep candidate and a red delete button (wording still to confirm). There is no Undo. Both are written to the change log. Risk accepted (flagged): two similar actions sit side by side, and Delete loses a recorded payment. **Still to confirm:** whether Delete permanently is also offered on a withdrawn row; the Removed group collapsed by default; refunds handled outside the dashboard for now (except clearing an overpaid amount, v0.166); the confirmation's button wording (Delete vs Remove). Rejected: A (delete only: a paid registration vanishes and mistakes can't be undone); B (withdraw only, no way to delete). |

| Star testing Grading: picking a grade (decided v0.125) | Option C (Jeffrey). Each row shows the candidate with their school, Testing for, **Total of 315**, and **one result tag** (rule 8) with its reason on a second line: green **Passed** ("Earns 1 star"), amber **Make-up** ("D in Breaking · retests Breaking", "Below 245 · retests …"), or gray **Not finished** ("Korean Terminology not graded yet"). A ⋯ menu ends each row. Rejected: B (grouped by result); C (all Dans in one list). |
| Star testing Results: results stay open (decided v0.139) | (See v0.173: too few classes attended is not a make-up reason.) Jeffrey, on Results Question 2: a testing "really never closes", because candidates who didn't pass make things up afterwards. There is **no "make results final" step** and **no lock**: staff and admins can update grades and class counts on an event at any time, and every change is logged (rule 5). This **replaces v0.127's lock** and the "Results final" screen in the Grading mockups (v0.137, screen 7). **Class make-ups:** when a candidate has done the missing classes or helping classes, staff update the count **on the same event**. A new Assistant classes count recalculates the Assistant Instructor grade, Total and Result, so a Make-up can become Passed. **Subject make-ups stay flexible** ("let us handle it"): staff decide case by case whether to grade the subject later on the same event or add the candidate to the next testing's make-up list (v0.111). The dashboard doesn't force either. Results go to families per candidate, whenever each is ready (how: Results Questions 3–4). **Still to confirm:** whether too few **classes attended** is itself a make-up reason (Jeffrey mentioned classes; v0.126 records classes attended as not changing the score); whether subject grades entered later on the same event show their date (e.g. "Breaking: B+, Dec 3"); how the make-up list (v0.111) knows a make-up was already done on the same event. Rejected: A (final only when everyone is finished); B (final any time with a confirmation); C (final per candidate). |

Jeffrey: **Option B now, Option A later** once the school has its own domain. **Now (B):** the dashboard writes each family's result email (to the candidate's email for results, with the result and its reason), and it **opens in the school's own email account** for staff to check and press Send. No email service and no cost. One Send per family; staff pressing Send in their own email is the rule 1.4 check. The dashboard can't see whether it was actually sent. **Later (A):** once the school has a domain, an email service (e.g. Resend, whose free plan should cover this volume; to check when building) sends results straight from the dashboard, with a confirmation naming who gets what (rule 1.4). The message wording carries over (Question 4). Why not A now: without a school domain, emails from a service are unreliable (spam or not delivered). **Still to confirm:** how the email is opened without putting a minor's name and result in a web address or browser history (rule 5) (answered v0.248); the message wording and how the dashboard records it (Question 4); where the email button sits; what happens on a device with no email account set up. Rejected: C (printed letters only). |

One click records **"Sent Nov 16 · Staff"** (the date and who; the shared desk account shows as "Staff", v0.27, a personal login shows the person's name). It takes effect at once with a 5-second Undo toast (rule 1.4). Rows not yet sent show **"Not sent yet"**, so late make-up results (v0.139) aren't missed. Marking as sent is logged (rule 5). When a sending service is added later (v0.140, A), it records "Sent" by itself and the Mark as sent click goes away. **Still to confirm:** the message wording drawn in the question (one message per result type, always with the total, the pass mark and, for a make-up, exactly what to work on); correcting a mistaken Mark as sent after the Undo toast; whether a re-sent result (e.g. after a make-up) keeps the earlier send date. Rejected: A (no record); B (records "Email opened" automatically, which isn't the same as sent). |

(Process: each design and security review checks it.) As v0.161, this is enforced on screen only, not by the database. **Flagged, Jeffrey's call:** every such screen needs a hidden version, and one missed screen would show a minor's details on that tablet. **Exception (v0.250):** Color Belt's "Not paid" tag on its Grading tab (v0.183) stays visible in mat-side mode. **Still to confirm (next questions):** who can turn the mode on and off (answered v0.251: anyone signed in), where the setting lives (answered v0.252: account menu), and what a new or reset tablet shows (answered v0.253: starts off). **Rejected:** A (Grading only: the tablet shows only the Star Grading tab). |

| Color Belt phase 4: build plan answers (decided v0.254; changes v0.228 and v0.136) | `TKD-Testing-Phase4-Build-Plan.md` §6, after an independent review. (1) **Price after Back to the testing day = the live rule: it depends on the day they pay** (early before the testing day, testing-day price on or after) — **v0.228’s “by her registration date” is corrected**. (2) **A paid private student’s belt or class corrected to a pricier rank is re-priced:** Edit details warns first (“Price $85 → $95 · $10 more to collect”), then the amber owed line shows and Mark paid records the difference; corrected to a cheaper rank → the payment stays, nothing owed, no refund. Regular students are unchanged (v0.136). (3) **Change how they’ll pay shows while a private student still owes money** (hidden again once nothing is owed); earlier payments keep their method. (4) **A private day may be the testing day itself** at another time (graded on her own page; the Private testings list shows her). (5) **For a private testing only a result counts as graded** — a note alone keeps her on the list and Back possible (phase 3’s own locks unchanged). (6) **The Color Belt mat-side hiding (Star v0.249/v0.250) is built by the Color Belt session**, as a follow-up once Star’s switch reaches `main` (phase 4 doesn’t wait). (7) Confirmed: after any testing day the desk may set an ungraded student private for today and grade them. From the review: after the testing day **only admins can send a private student back to the testing day** (so a cleared No show can’t vanish); a **private No show can get a new day** (clears it, logged); the keep-one / restore lock and the event-date lock count private days; signatures are deleted **90 days after the later of the testing day and the private day** (changes v0.136 wording); icons `ti-calendar` (Set / Change private), `ti-calendar-event` (Back to the testing day), `ti-school` (Grade private testing). Add time slot can pick several days at once; removing a slot is instant with Undo; overlapping classes get an amber note but still save. **Class colors:** the admin picks each class's color in the time slot dialog from 8 soft colors (gray, yellow, orange, green, teal, sky, purple, pink; dark text on a pale fill); it applies to every slot of that class; **red is never offered**; new classes start gray. Closures are **amber with `ti-alert-triangle`** (a warning: classes cancelled); cancelled classes stay visible **crossed out**; Week view stripes the closed hours amber; cancelled classes can't be booked. **Events:** title, type, all day or start/end times, can run several days, Worcester / Shrewsbury / Both, notes under "More details". **Event color comes from its type**; admins manage types under `⋯` → Event types (name + one of the 8 class colors, no red; shared by both schools; recoloring a type recolors its events; deleting a type in use asks to confirm and where its events move). Clicking a day opens it in a **side panel** on PC (shows today until another day is clicked; lists events, free classes with name/time/class, and every class in its color); **tablet held upright** stacks the month above the chosen day; **phone** shows a small month with colored dots and the chosen day listed underneath. **Week view** (one click away; PC and tablet only) uses the Schedule look (times on the side, box height = length) with real dates, an "All day" row, events with a thicker left edge, and a `ti-user-plus` count badge on classes with free-class bookings. Clicking a row opens a **side panel** (tablet upright: below the list; phone: full screen): Call / Text / Email (open the device's own apps; PC shows the number large with Copy; nothing is sent by the dashboard), status, follow-up date, free class, siblings, **notes and calls** (note + one-tap follow-up: Tomorrow, In 3 days, Next week, Pick a date, No follow-up; saved with who and when; notes are not editable), source form with all answers and the signed waiver. Built-in ones (New, Booked, Came, Joined, No-show, Not interested) can be renamed but not deleted, because the app uses them: a sign-up without a booking starts New, with one Booked; Joined is never auto-deleted; Not interested is deleted after 2 years. Choosing Joined asks the date joined (default today); Joined is the place for families who signed up until a Students section exists. **Deleting a prospect is admins only**, with a confirmation. **Phone bottom bar** (six sections now): Home, Calendar, Prospects, More (`ti-menu-2`: Testing, Schedule, Forms); PC and tablet rails show all six. |

(3) **Progress track only in the List and the panel** (choice A): each List row gets a "Progress" column — the small track (Signed up → Class booked → Came → Joined; green = done, blue ring = next, gray = not yet; a side exit shows where they stopped) plus one line; the panel gets the big track with the date under each step and a "Next:" line. **Board cards stay plain** — the column already says the stage. Recorded flaw (Jeffrey's call): on touch screens, dragging can be confused with scrolling and a drop can land in the wrong column. |

| Rulebook version numbers: one block per branch (decided v0.300) | Option A (Jeffrey, 2026-10-19). Several branches add rulebook rows at the same time, and "take the next free number" kept clashing (on 2026-10-19: 0.209 and 0.216 on finance + black-belt, 0.233–0.237 on camps + new students). From now on **each branch uses only its own block of numbers**: `main` / Main session **0.300–0.399**, `color-belt` **0.400–0.499**, `star-testing` **0.500–0.599**, new students (`claude/prospect-form-system-cd893d`) **0.600–0.699**, `camps` **0.700–0.799**, `finance` **0.800–0.899**, `black-belt` **0.900–0.999**; a new branch gets the next free block (1.000+) after asking Jeffrey, and a branch that runs out asks for another. New rows take the next unused number in the branch's block, after `git merge main`. **Rows numbered before this rule keep their numbers until that branch merges into `main`; at that merge only the rows that clash with a number already on `main` are renumbered into the branch's own block** (10b row, changelog line, and "vX.XX" mentions in that branch's docs). Consequences accepted: version numbers no longer show the order of decisions across branches (the changelog date does), and the changelog has gaps; the check is "no number used twice". Rule written in `CLAUDE.md` ("Parallel sessions"). Rejected: B, keep "next free number" and renumber at every merge. |

| Camps: extras on chosen days — pizza days (decided v0.705) | Jeffrey at the CP1 walkthrough, 2026-10-19: a week camp has pizza on specific days (e.g. Wednesday and Friday) and families must see which days. A **per-day extra** can be limited to camp days: Add / Edit camp → 4. Extras → **Only on these days** (camp-day chips, dark outline; none = every day; templates keep the weekdays). Charging is **optional** (Jeffrey chose it over automatic, and confirmed: all days = included; some days = the parent may tick it): the extra can only be chosen on the child's days within those days; "Not charged to all-days campers" works as before. Check in shows "Pizza party today: N" (campers who chose it that day + all-days campers) and a "No pizza party" note for a child coming that day without it. Narrowing an extra's days while an active camper chose a removed day is **refused** ("Can't remove Wed from Pizza party: 3 campers chose it. Change their pizza party days first."), like v0.701. Picture `docs/Camps/Decision images/Camps-pizza-days-options.png`. |
| Camps: check-in on any day (decided v0.706; changes v0.704) | Jeffrey at the CP1 walkthrough, 2026-10-19: "make it so we can check in no matter what. Regardless of the day." Staff and admins can check in and undo a check-in on **any** camp day (past, today or later). Replaces v0.704's "check in works on today; fixing an earlier day is admin-only; future days have no button". Risk accepted: a check-in on the wrong day shows as "came" in Attendance; the Check in tab still opens on today during camp week (otherwise the next camp day), the summary line starts with the selected date, and Undo is always there. The camp page still opens on Check in only during camp week, otherwise on Payments. |
| Finance section + Daily report (decided v0.804) | Jeffrey (2026-10-18): families now pay for Color belt and Star testing, so the dashboard gets a **Finance** section (its own rail icon) and its **first tab is the Daily report**. Later tabs only when Jeffrey adds them (P7). Designed one question at a time with pictures (`docs/Finance/TKD-Finance-DailyReport-Questions.html`); 8 questions planned: what it does, who can open it, layout, picking the day, location / Both, undone payments, print / export, icon and Home. Reads the existing payment records (`testing_payments`, 0005; Star's once built). Nothing built yet. |
| Daily report: what it does (decided v0.805) | Option B (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18; he first said A, then changed to B). The Daily report shows the day's totals (all, cash, check, card, each with its count) and every payment (time, student, what it was for, how paid, amount, who recorded it), with arrows and a calendar to move to another day and **Print** — plus a **Close the day** box: staff type the **cash counted in the drawer**, the **number of checks** and the **card machine's total**; each shows **Matches** or **Short / Over $X** (meaning in text, not color alone), a short or over needs a **note**, and **Close the day** saves the count with the person's name and time. A payment added to a day after it was closed is flagged. Needs a new table and security-definer functions (handoff §6). **Still to decide:** who can close and who can reopen a closed day; days with no payments; whether checks are counted by number or amount; where admins see the history of closed days. Rejected: A (read only — doesn't catch a missing $20, no record of who checked the day), C (B + bank deposit tracking — more to build; only if the school deposits per day). |
| Finance roles (decided v0.806) | Option B (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). **Staff** (incl. the shared front-desk account, logged as “Staff”) see Finance with **today only** — today’s payments and the Close the day box, no arrows or calendar (“Staff see today only. Ask an admin for other days.”) — and can **close today**. **Admins** see **any day**, the **history of closed days**, can close any open day and can **reopen** a closed day (logged; the earlier count stays in the history). Enforced in the database, not only hidden on screen (handoff §6). Risk accepted (flagged): a close made on the shared desk account is logged as “Staff”, not a person; a day staff forgot to close has to be closed by an admin. Rejected: A (admins only — an admin would have to be there every closing), C (staff see every past day too). |
| Daily report: payments list grouped by how paid (decided v0.807) | Option B (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). Below the totals and the Close the day box, the day's payments are listed in **three groups in drawer-count order: Cash, Check, Card**. Each group has a header with its **count and total** (e.g. “Cash · 4 payments · $320.00”), then its payments in time order. PC columns: Time, Student, For (testing type · event date), Amount, Recorded by (Paid by is the group). Phones: stacked cards under the same group headers. A group with no payments is left out. Why: staff count the cash against the Cash group and lay the checks next to the Check group, so a short is easier to trace. Rejected: A (one time-ordered list), C (grouped by testing event — doesn't help the drawer count). |
| Daily report: moving between days + History (decided v0.197) | Option C (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). **Admins** move day to day with **‹ ›** arrows, a **calendar** to jump to any date and **Today** to come back. A **History** button (`ti-history`, new in the icon map when built) opens a list of one month's days, with **‹ ›** to change the month and **Back to the day**: each row shows the day, its total, who closed it and when, and **one tag** (Closed · Cash short / over $X · Not closed · No payments · Today · open; warnings first, rule 8). Clicking a row opens that day's report. Phones: stacked rows, back arrow. Staff never see the arrows, calendar or History (today only, v0.806). Rejected: A (no overview — unclosed days found one by one), B (marks inside the calendar — small, need a legend, no names or amounts). |
| Daily report on Both (decided v0.198) | Option C (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). On **Worcester** or **Shrewsbury** the Daily report shows that school only. On **Both** it shows **one box per school**: total and number of payments, cash · check · card, one tag (Closed / Not closed yet) and an **Open [school]** button, plus a line with the combined total. There is **no payment list and no Close the day on Both**. **Open [school]** switches the top bar (the whole dashboard) to that school and shows its **full** Daily report (totals, Close the day, Cash / Check / Card list, Print, History) — Jeffrey confirmed this is what he expects; going back to the summary means picking Both again. A Star testing payment counts for the **candidate's school** (Star events are joint, v0.58). Each school closes its own drawer. **To confirm in the full mockups:** History on Both (proposed: both schools' days with a School column). Rejected: A (no money on Both, like Testing v0.35 — no combined total), B (combined totals + School column — busier). |
| Daily report: undone and edited payments (decided v0.199) | Option B (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). Corrections show on the **payment's original day**, even though the database dates an undo on the day it is made. An **undone** payment stays in its group **struck through** with a gray **Undone** tag and “Undone by <name> · <date> · not counted”. An **edited** payment shows its new amount and method with an **Edited** tag and “Was $70.00 · edited by <name> · <date>”. **Totals count only what is true now.** A closed day that changes afterwards gets an amber **Changed after closing** tag (with `ti-alert-triangle` and text), both on the day and in History; its box says how many payments changed, when and by whom, and that the totals no longer match the count. Why: testing fees aren't refunded, so an undo almost always fixes a mistake on that day. **To confirm in the full mockups:** a staff mis-click undone within its 10 seconds is hidden, not struck through (proposed); an edit that changes the payment's date shows on the new day, with a “Moved to <date>” line on the old one (proposed). Rejected: A (corrections hidden — the report shows that something changed but not what), C (counted on the day the correction is made — Monday's cash wouldn't match Monday's drawer). |
| Daily report: print and export (decided v0.200) | Option C (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). **Print** (`ti-printer`; staff print today, admins any day) makes a one-page letter-size copy of the day: school, date, who printed it and when, the four totals, the Close the day count and note, the Cash / Check / Card groups, and a **“Counted by: ______” line to sign**. **Export…** (`ti-download`, **admins only**) opens a small dialog: **From** and **To** dates (default: this month), the school from the top bar, a line with the count and total, then **Download CSV** — one row per payment (date, school, time, student, for, event date, paid by, amount, recorded by, status), with corrections marked in the status column. **Every download is logged** (who, when, range, school), because the file holds students' names and amounts. Phones: Print and Export sit under a ⋯ menu next to the date. Rejected: A (print only — a bookkeeper retypes the numbers), B (export one day — a month takes ~30 downloads). |
| Finance rail icon and place (decided v0.201) | Option A (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). Finance's icon is **`ti-report-money`** (a clipboard with a dollar sign). It sits **last on the rail**, after the sections other branches add (Schedule, Calendar, Forms, Prospects); on **phones it goes under More** (the bottom bar is already Home, Calendar, Prospects, More, v0.140). Add it to the §2.4 icon map when built. Risk accepted (flagged): at 18px it looks a lot like Forms' `ti-clipboard-text`; the hover / touch label says Finance. Because the icon now means Finance, the report's **Total** card must use a different icon (proposed `ti-sum`; Cash `ti-cash`, Check `ti-receipt`, Card `ti-credit-card` to confirm in the full mockups). Rejected: B (`ti-coins`), C (`ti-cash-banknote` — too close to the Cash card's `ti-cash`). |
| Finance on Home: close-the-day reminder (decided v0.202) | Option C (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). Finance puts **no dollar amounts on Home** (rule 5: Home is seen on the mat-side tablet). Instead, on a day the school took payments and **hasn't closed yet**, from the **reminder time** onward Home shows an amber line with `ti-alert-triangle`: “Today's drawer isn't closed yet. **Close the day** ›” (opens the Daily report). It goes away once the day is closed. Admins also see how many **earlier days weren't closed** (opens History). The Home empty line goes away while the reminder shows. **The reminder time is a setting in Finance** (Jeffrey, same day) — how it works is the next question. Rejected: A (nothing on Home — no reminder to close), B (today's total on Home — money on the mat-side tablet). |
| Finance → Settings: reminder times (decided v0.203) | Option B (Jeffrey, 2026-10-18). Finance gets a second tab, **Settings**, that **only admins see** (staff keep just Daily report; enforced in the database). | Finance CP2: migration 0011 live (v0.802) | Jeffrey applied `0011_finance.sql` to production on 2026-10-19 (CP2). The first version was installed correctly but its tests could not finish on production (cancelled by the SQL Editor's time limit): payment chains were built with one query that grew very slow over long date ranges. A **speed fix** was built (owner-by-owner chain builder + `paid_on` indexes on `testing_payments` and `star_payments`; every Finance function under 60 ms on ~11,000 payments), a security review caught a **collation bug** in it (production sorts text with ICU en_US, PGlite with C — an end marker would have sorted first and emptied every Finance screen) — fixed, tested on an ICU database, re-reviewed “safe to paste” — and Jeffrey re-applied 0011 over the first version. Checks on production: all 95 fingerprint lines identical to the tested copy; every non-Finance function, table, trigger and policy unchanged; security advisor only the expected notices; settings and the 14 reminder rows untouched; **`0011_tests.sql`: ALL 0011 TESTS PASSED**. Rule for future migrations: never base logic on how text sorts; seed thousands of rows to catch slowness. Next: rerun the older test files, then CP3 (local check against the real database). |

Close the day with real money is first used after deploy (its database side passed in `0011_tests.sql` on production). Before this, the seven older test files (0010, 0006, 0005, 0004, 0003, 0002, PIN) were rerun on production after 0011: all passed. Next: CP4 — the Main session merges `finance` into `main` (renumbering Finance's old rows that clash with `main`) and, with Jeffrey's OK, pushes (= deploy). |

| Color Belt phase 4: private testing money in Finance’s Daily report (decided v0.407) | Found when merging Finance (live, migration 0011) into color-belt (2026-10-19): Finance’s Daily report reads Color Belt event payments (`testing_payments`) and Star payments, but **not private testing payments** (`testing_private_payments`, migration 0014). Without a fix, money taken for a private testing would be missing from the day’s totals and the cash count. Jeffrey: **A — the Finance session adds private testing payments to the Daily report (totals, payments list, history, export, payment detail) as a new migration (0018 — 0016 is Black Belt, 0017 is camps; 0018 runs after 0017 and must build on 0017’s versions of the shared finance_ functions; renumbered from 0017 on 2026-10-19 by Jeffrey, CLAUDE.md on main 8ff2ee7), the Color Belt session reviews it, and Color Belt phase 4 deploys only after it is live.** A Move keeps each payment’s original day on the private side (event side reversed on the same day), so a day’s totals don’t change once both sources are read; Finance must count a move’s event-side reversal as a transfer, not a refund. Applying 0014 to the database (CP2) is not blocked: no private payments exist until the screens are deployed. |
| Session id in the history log can’t be planted through the public forms (decided v0.408) | Found by Black Belt’s 0016 security review (2026-10-20), verified on production: Supabase’s `auth.jwt()` reads the legacy `request.jwt.claim` setting before `request.jwt.claims`; the public form functions (`testing_public_register` 0005, `star_public_register` 0006, `forms_public_submit` / `forms_public_email_result` 0009, camps’ form in 0013) clear only `request.jwt.claims` and `request.jwt.claim.sub`. `public.current_session_id()` (0001) reads the session id through `auth.jwt()` and is stamped into every `change_log` row, so whoever holds the `testing_registrar` password could make a form-written history row show a staff session id. No access gain (`auth.uid()` / `has_data_access()` stay empty) — low severity. Jeffrey: **B — one migration (number from the Main session) changes `current_session_id()` to read the session id only from `request.jwt.claims`** (what Supabase’s API server sets for a signed-in user), never the legacy setting, so every current and future public form is covered without touching the form functions. Because the PIN lock and server lock use this function, it ships with tests and **PIN entry, lock / unlock and a history row are checked on the live site right after it is applied**. Written by the Color Belt session; security-reviewed by a separate agent before Jeffrey pastes it. |

| SETUP.md sections named by migration number (decided v0.302) | Jeffrey, 2026-10-20, on the Color Belt session's suggestion. Branches kept giving their `supabase/SETUP.md` sections the next free number, and they clashed like the rulebook versions did (on 2026-10-20 three branches used **§18**: camps for 0013, Finance for 0018, Black Belt for 0016). From now on **a section for a migration is titled by its migration number**: `## Migration 00NN: <what it does>` with sub-steps `### 00NNa.`, `### 00NNb.` … (e.g. Color Belt's `§0014`, `### 0014e.`). Migration numbers are already unique (CLAUDE.md table), so these can't clash. A section that isn't about one migration (for example a Cloudflare setup) gets a **descriptive title with no number**. **Sections already on `main` (§1–§17) keep their numbers**, because the test runner finds some of them by number (e.g. `### 14e.`); a branch whose unmerged section clashes renames it to its migration number before or at its merge, and updates every "SETUP §…" mention and any `run.mjs` lookup with it. Rule written in `CLAUDE.md` ("Parallel sessions"). |
| Database test steps named by migration number (decided v0.304) | Jeffrey, 2026-10-20 (extends v0.302). The PGlite test runner `supabase/tests/pglite/run.mjs` groups its checks into step functions, and branches kept taking "the next free" step number, so they clashed (on 2026-10-20 `step19` meant 0020 on `color-belt` and school phone on `finance`, which had renamed main's `step18`). From now on **a step for a migration is named after its migration number: `step00NN`** (e.g. `step0020`); extra checks for the same migration use `step00NN_<what>` (e.g. `step0017_checks`). A step that isn't about one migration gets a unique descriptive name. **Steps already on `main` (`step11`–`step18`) keep their names**; unique descriptive names already used on branches (`stepCamps`, `stepFinanceCamps`, `stepBlackBelt`) may stay. A branch whose unmerged step uses a plain number renames it to `step00NN` before or at its merge, and never renames a step that came from `main`. Rule written in `CLAUDE.md` ("Parallel sessions"). |
| Star family form: built and approved in preview (decided v0.511) | (See v0.410: `RATE_LIMITER` is **not** a go-live step for any public form — Pages Functions can't have the binding; revisit with a WAF rule once the school has its own domain.) Jeffrey tried Star phase 4 in preview on 2026-10-20 (the public `/star/<code>` page, its Cloudflare Function and the preview fake; registrations appear on the Candidates tab), after a full security review of the Function and of the page and a design review, and approved it with these calls: (1) **Defaults OK:** registering on the testing day itself shows the testing-day price ("$150 today"); when a Dan's two prices match, the fee shows once; an empty email says "Enter an email for results."; changing the Dan keeps the stars choice if it still fits, else clears it; the Assistant Instructor line shows only when a subject has that name; "Opens a calendar." is hidden while the calendar is open; a malformed server answer counts as "didn't load". (2) **The signature box keeps Color Belt's screen-reader name** ("Signature box. (3) **No "another candidate" note** in the step line after Register another (Form screen 10's text was a mockup note). **Build notes:** while the Star switch is off, live `/star` and `/star/<code>` show a plain static "This registration isn't open yet" page (`star-closed.html`, no script) — a build-time rewrite sends them to the form only when the switch is on; the Cloudflare `RATE_LIMITER` binding was made a required go-live step (SETUP 13i) — **dropped by v0.410**; the Function checks the signature is a real PNG and copies the school address's four parts only. **Go-live checklist (status file):** Master Jeong reads the acknowledgment; both street addresses in Settings → Schools; SETUP 13e, 13f and 13i; a TEST registration before `STAR_PUBLIC_REGISTRATION` is switched on. |

Asked because the icon map rule says a new icon is added before it is used; the Main session adds it to the §2.4 map when color-belt merges. |
| Black Belt money in the Daily report (decided v0.808) | Jeffrey, 2026-10-19 (question from the Black Belt testing session): **Black Belt testing payments belong in the school's Daily report**, even though Black Belt checks are made out to Master Jeong personally. When Black Belt payments are built (`bb_…` table, branch `black-belt`), Finance adds them to its read functions: kinds payment / reversal / refund / returned check; each row's **school as recorded** (not the candidate's current school); “For” line “Black Belt · <cycle> · <what it's for>” (e.g. “Black Belt · Spring 2027 · Payment 2”); amounts above $1,000 (4th Dan fee $2,700). Same day rule as Color Belt and Star (counts on the day recorded, incl. post-dated checks). How a **returned (bounced) check** shows is still open. Nothing changes in Finance until Black Belt payments exist. |
| Daily report: bounced (returned) checks (decided v0.809) | Option B (Jeffrey, 2026-10-19; Black Belt testing question). A check the bank returns (bounced) shows on the **day it was returned** (the day staff record “Returned check”), in its own **Returned checks** group at the end of the list: the student, −amount, a warning-style **Returned check** tag and “Returned by the bank · handed in <date> · recorded by <name>”. That day's Total shows the net (“after a $900.00 returned check”). **The day the check was handed in stays exactly as counted** (still closed and matching) — this is an exception to v0.199's “corrections on the original day”, because a bounce isn't a recording mistake. **Close the day is not affected** on either day (no cash or check moves in the drawer). The export lists the returned check as its own row. Applies when Black Belt's returned-check rows exist (v0.808); Color Belt and Star have no returned checks today. Rejected: A (on the handed-in day, which would mark a correctly counted day Changed after closing weeks later and show nothing on the day it bounced). |
| Finance: what counts as “changed after closing” (decided v0.810) | Option A (Jeffrey, 2026-10-20; raised by the security review of migration 0018). From 0018 on, a closed day is compared with what is true now **by each payment's history (its first payment), its amount, method and kind** — not by the newest database row. So only **real money changes** mark a closed day **Changed after closing**: a payment added, undone, deleted, or changed in amount, method or day (and a Star school change). An admin's **Edit payment saved without changing anything** (same amount, method and day) **no longer flags** the day, for every source (Color Belt, Star, camps, private testing); the edit still shows in the payment's history and the change log. This is also what keeps a **Move to private testing** from flagging a day (a transfer, v0.407). Rejected: B (keep flagging no-op edits — more complex and has to work around moves). |
| Home: “OK” for the earlier-unclosed-days reminder (decided v0.811) | Option B (Jeffrey, 2026-10-20). The admin's Home line “N earlier days at <school> weren't closed, the first on <day>. Open History ›” (v0.202 / v0.207) gets an **OK** button. OK **remembers which unclosed days (school + day) were seen**, on this device / browser (like the default location); the line then stays away while only those days are unclosed. When a **new** earlier day isn't closed, the line comes back for the new days only, e.g. “1 new earlier day at Worcester wasn't closed: Mon, Oct 19 (21 seen before).” with Open History and OK again. Closing an old day later works as before; History is unchanged (still “Not closed”). Today's own reminder (“Today's drawer isn't closed yet”, staff and admins) is **not** affected. No database change. Seen days older than the 30-day window are forgotten. Rejected: A (hide until tomorrow — keeps nagging daily), C (mark days “Won't be closed” in History — needs a migration after 0017 / 0018; can be added later). |
| Daily report: icon for the refunds switch in the phone ⋯ menu (decided v0.812) | Option A (Jeffrey, 2026-10-20; gap found by the Main session). The ⋯ menu line “Refunds: show in groups / show as own group” (phone layout, only on days with refunds; v0.209) uses **`ti-receipt-refund`** (a receipt with a back-arrow), as already built. Rejected: `ti-arrows-sort` (means Sort in the map) and `ti-list` (means List view on Prospects) — reusing them would give one icon two meanings. The Main session adds it to the 2.4 icon map. Picture: `docs/Finance/images/Finance-Q18-refund-icon.png`. |
| Public forms: flood protection without a Rate Limiting binding (decided v0.410) | Found 2026-10-20/21 (Star’s security review of the copied `register.js`): the public Functions’ optional `RATE_LIMITER` binding isn’t set on production, and **Cloudflare Pages Functions can’t have one** (the Rate Limiting binding is Workers-only); WAF rate-limiting rules need a domain on Cloudflare, which `*.pages.dev` isn’t. Jeffrey: **A — accept the current protections for now** for every public form (Color Belt, Star, Forms): the per-server memory limiter (60 page loads a minute per visitor per Cloudflare server), Turnstile on every Send, the registrar logins’ connection and time limits, and the off switches (`PUBLIC_REGISTRATION` etc.) as the response to a flood. No data is at risk; a flood can only make a form unavailable. **Revisit with a WAF rate-limiting rule on the form addresses when the school has its own domain on Cloudflare.** No form treats the binding as a go-live requirement. |

A mouse keeps the desktop sizes at any width. One CSS block in `src/styles/register.css` (`@media (pointer: coarse)`); checked in preview at a 767px touch width. |

Phones keep one candidate at a time (v0.137); PC and the sideways tablet keep the full grid. Rejected: A (one candidate at a time on the upright tablet), C (a "Turn the tablet sideways" message, against rule 1.3). **Changes to approved decisions:** (1) Result wording on Grading = the Results tab's words — the approved drawing's **"Passing" becomes "Passed"** (v0.137, v0.138); (2) **"N of M left" counts Assistant Instructor** for a normal candidate, so it matches Results' "Not finished" reason (2nd Dan's "7 of 12 left" becomes "8 of 13 left"); a make-up keeps the approved meaning (retest subjects, "1 of 2 left"); (3) the selected cell (2px blue inset) and a make-up's retest cells (1px blue outline) are a **named exception to §1.2's 5-blue limit for the grading grid**. |
| Prospects: a No-show stays on the Board (decided v0.610) | **Jeffrey, 2026-10-21** (after trying the live system: the List showed 8 children and the Board 7 — the No-show had no column; image `docs/Prospects/Prospects-noshow-on-board.png`): **keep No-shows on the screens until staff remove them; nothing takes them off by itself.** A No-show is a card in the **Booked** column (the stage it reached) with the amber line **"Didn't show — call back"**; the Booked tile counts it and, in the List, shows it with the Booked children; the List already shows it with everyone. It leaves the Board only when staff change its status or delete it; letting its card go on the Booked column again changes nothing (to book a new class, open the card). The No-show chip still shows just the No-shows, so a No-show is counted both in the Booked tile and in the chip. The status itself keeps its name **No-show** (tag, chip, status menu, history); only the card line and the toast say "Didn't show". The 2-year clean-up (v0.244) is unchanged (option 2, also exempting No-shows from it, was not chosen). Not interested: still under its chip — to be decided. |
| Prospects: Not interested families deleted after 6 months (decided v0.611; changes v0.244) | **Jeffrey, 2026-10-21** ("what happens after months of people not being interested?"; image `docs/Prospects/Prospects-notinterested-sooner.png`): **a family in which every child is Not interested is deleted automatically 6 months after its last activity** (a note, a call, a status change, a booking or a check-in — marking someone Not interested is itself activity, so the 6 months start then at the latest). Every other family without a Joined child keeps the **2 years** of v0.244; Joined is never deleted. A family with one Not interested child and a brother still in a stage keeps the 2 years. Not interested children stay **under their chip** (off the Board) and in search until then; **Delete prospect** (admins only, asks first) removes anyone at once — for a mistake, a test entry, or a parent who asks. Rejected: 3 months ("maybe in spring" families gone too soon), 1 year (twice as many kept). Database: **migration 0021** (`0021_prospects_not_interested_sooner.sql`, number reserved by Main) replaces only `prospects_purge_old`; once it is live, families already past 6 months go at the next Prospects load. Never re-run 0012 or 0015 after it. |
| One meaning for `ti-cash`: cash only (decided v0.303) | Option A (Jeffrey, 2026-10-20). `ti-cash` was used for two things (rule 2.3 "same icon = same meaning"): the **Payments recorded** card (Color Belt's event page, live; Camps' roster, recorded v0.229) — a dollar total of cash, check and card payments — and Finance's **Cash** card (v0.207), cash only. Now **`ti-cash` means cash only**, and every **Payments recorded** card uses **`ti-sum`**, the same icon as Finance's **Total**, so `ti-sum` means "a money total" everywhere. Code to change: Color Belt `src/screens/testing/event/EventPage.jsx` (the Payments recorded card; live, so it needs a deploy), the Camps roster card, and Star's / Black Belt's Payments recorded tiles when built. Camps' icon-map row "Payments recorded card … `ti-cash`" (v0.229) is replaced by the `ti-sum` row when camps merges. Rejected: B, keep `ti-cash` on both as "money / cash" (the Cash card would read as all money next to Total, Check and Card); C, change Finance's Cash card to `ti-cash-banknote` (changes the approved v0.207 design). |

The family handout, emails, certificates and online card payments are planned for later. **Plan approved:** `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-Plan.md`, checked by two reviewer agents (status file §4). It is for the **next cycle (Spring 2027)**, not Fall 2026, which finishes in the old system. Registration and payments are live by about early February 2027, grading by the first testing day. Design runs in two tracks: registration and payment questions start now; grading questions wait for Master Jeong's answers on the rules (plan §9 part A). The pattern is Star testing's: separate tables, its own public-form login, results worked out each time. Proposed in the plan and decided one at a time: the grading tablet gets only the age bracket, never the date of birth; the payment ledger has its own cap and kinds (returned check, credit); the school and cycle are stored on each payment. Nothing built yet. |
| Black Belt testing: built now, not queued (decided v0.205) | Jeffrey (2026-10-18): **Black Belt is being built now**, in its own worktree session, alongside the other sections. The cycle also holds the deadlines (application due, submissions due) and the payment due dates. Add cycle starts as a copy of the last cycle's days, deadlines and due dates. Admins add and edit cycles (roles: question B4). Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-Cycle-Questions.html`. **To settle with the mockups:** (1) Save is refused while a part is graded on no day; (2) a part may be graded over two days (split curriculum), and grades from both days count as one; (3) an outdoor part is a second time and place on the same day, or a day of its own; (4) whether a day can be removed once it has grades. Rejected: three fixed days (A); a short Add cycle with details filled in later (C). |
| Black Belt testing roles (decided v0.210) | Jeffrey chose **A, same as Star** (2026-10-18). **Admins only:** edit the Rubric and prices tab; add or edit a cycle and its testing days, deadlines and payment due dates; open registration after the closing date; edit or undo a payment after the 5-second toast; record a refund; mark a check returned (bounced); change the plan or the Kukkiwon certificate once something is paid; remove a real candidate. **Staff** (incl. the shared desk account, logged as "Staff") **and admins:** view the rubric and prices (staff: "View only. Ask an admin to make changes."); close registration any time, and open it only before the closing date (Star v0.86); add a candidate or fix their details; change the plan or the Kukkiwon certificate before anything is paid; record a payment (with the 5-second Undo); enter grades on any testing day and change them later (logged; results stay open, v0.139); print results, grading sheets and receipts. Date of birth and email appear only in a single candidate's detail view on desk devices. The grading tablet shows the age bracket only, and payments never appear on grading screens. **Enforced in the database**, not just hidden on the screen. Rejected: B (only admins record payments); C (staff also move testing days, reopen late, and fix payments later). Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-Roles-Questions.html`. |
| Black Belt testing registration form: fields (decided v0.211) | Jeffrey chose **A, like Star's form** (2026-10-18). A parent's email is fine."). **School comes from the link** (one link per school, Star v0.98). **Not asked:** sex, phone, or a parent's name or email (a parent signs on step 2, question B7). Every field is checked again in the database. **To confirm:** add sex back only if Kukkiwon needs it (Master Jeong question A12); the exact error messages follow Star's. | Black Belt testing: who may register (decided v0.212) | Jeffrey chose **A, open form, staff check the list** (2026-10-18). Anyone with a school's link can register for any Dan. Staff look over new registrations and fix a wrong Dan or details (B4); admins remove a fake or wrong registration; possible duplicates are flagged (Star pattern). The form **closes automatically at the end of the application date** (11:59 PM Massachusetts time, the v0.85 switch). Staff can close it any time; only admins reopen it after that date (B4). **Late families:** staff add the candidate at the desk at any time, or an admin reopens the form for everyone. Rejected: B (an admin approves each registration); C (only invited candidates, each with a personal link). Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-Form-Questions.html` (B6). |
| Black Belt testing: waiver step and keeping signed waivers (decided v0.213) | Jeffrey chose **A** (2026-10-18). **Step 2 · Check and send** of the Black Belt form shows: the summary (cycle with its first testing day, candidate, school from the link, date of birth with the age on the first testing day, Dan and Kukkiwon, the amounts to pay with their due dates, where results go); the **school's waiver text**, with a version number and "Read all"; an **"I have read and agree to the waiver."** tick; **Name of the person signing**; and the drawn **signature**. **A parent or guardian signs when the candidate is under 18 on the first testing day.** Admins edit the waiver text on the Rubric and prices tab. A change makes a new version, and old registrations keep theirs. A family who can't sign on the phone signs on paper at the desk, and staff tick "signed on paper". **Keeping:** the waiver releases the school from injury claims, so the signed copy (signature, signer's name, text version, date) is **kept for a set number of years, then deleted automatically**. This differs from Color Belt's and Star's 90 days. The number of years is an admin setting; **Jeffrey asks the school's insurer for it** (planning example: 3 years after the last testing day, or until 3 years after an under-18 turns 18, whichever is later). Only admins can open a signature. Signatures never appear in lists, on the grading tablet, in exports or in the change log ("on file" only). | Black Belt testing: confirmation page and requirements (decided v0.919) | Jeffrey chose **compact A** (2026-10-18). **Requirements:** step 1 of the form has a single line, **"What [Dan] is tested on ›"**, in the total box. The panel shows only the **parts**, one row each with its testing day, place and number of subjects, then **Hand in by [submissions date]**, **Extra points**, and the **pass rule** (pass mark and no D). Tapping a part shows its subjects with their credits; tapping a subject shows its scoring, e.g. the A+…C cut-offs per age group for a physical event. **It is filled from the cycle's own rubric and testing days**, the same data the grading uses, so it can't disagree with grading. **After sending**, the confirmation page shows: "[Name] is registered"; **What to pay** (each due date with its amount, the Kukkiwon fee with payment 1, the note on bringing all post-dated checks by the first date, and that fees are non-refundable); **Testing days** (date, part, time, place, the outdoor part) and the hand-in deadline; the **Kukkiwon steps** (only if chosen: Tcon account, give the desk the Tcon user ID, upload a photo); the same **requirements panel**; a **reference number** (BB-####); **Print or save this page**; and Register another candidate. This stands in for today's confirmation email until the dashboard can send email. **The old Worker page and `requirements.html` can be switched off** once Black Belt is live (its own step, with Jeffrey's OK). From the day after (e.g. Mar 28 for Mar 27) the tag turns red **Not paid** and counts under Not paid. On the due date itself the tag is still gray. Rows with something paid follow v0.219 as before. | Black Belt testing: cycles, family form and Candidates/payments mockups approved (decided v0.900) | Jeffrey went through all 26 screens one at a time on 2026-10-18/19. The three files `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-Cycles-Mockups.html` (8 screens), `TKD-BlackBelt-Form-Mockups.html` (9) and `TKD-BlackBelt-Candidates-Mockups.html` (9), built from `TKD-BlackBelt-Mockups-Brief.md` and checked by a design reviewer, are **approved as drawn, including every amber proposal**. The list of each screen and what it settles is in `TKD-BlackBelt-Status.md` §3b. **Cycles and setup:** the Testing chooser gets a third row "Black Belt testing" with the `ti-award` medal icon and a "Next: [next testing day] · both schools" line. Cycle status tags: Not open yet → Open until [date] → Registration closed → Next day [date] → Testing today; a cycle moves to Past the day after its last testing day. Delete cycle (admins) only while no payment is recorded. Add cycle starts from Fall 2026's schedule (loaded once from the Sheet), and after that each cycle copies the last one. Each plan has an "Offer this plan" tick, and payment 1's date follows Application due until changed by hand. **The four v0.918 testing-day details:** (1) Save is refused while a part is graded on no day; (2) a part may be graded on two days, and the grades count as one; (3) an outdoor part is a "second part" (time and place) of the same day; (4) a day, or a ticked part, that already has grades can't be deleted or unticked. Rubric and prices: plans are dragged into the order the form shows; waiver versions are named like "2027-1"; signed waivers are kept N years after the last testing day (under-18s until 3 years after they turn 18, if later), with N from the insurer. **Registration links: one link and QR code per school, plus Download QR code (a PNG), available at any time (open, not open yet or closed) for staff and admins (Jeffrey).** No flyer for now; a `?dan=1–4` hint; new random link codes each cycle. The cycle page has the summary "First day … · N testing days · both schools · N candidates" and a ⋯ menu (Edit cycle, Registration links, Open/Close registration). **Family form:** the cycle box shows dates and places only; the Kukkiwon fee and plan amounts appear once a Dan is chosen; Kukkiwon starts on "No, thanks"; each plan is a two-line card (name + total; payments + dates); the total box shows the chosen plan's schedule with Kukkiwon in payment 1. The requirements panel is a dialog on tablet and PC, with credits per subject and a maximum per part (extra-point numbers wait for Master Jeong). New messages: "Choose the Dan the candidate is testing for.", "Choose how you'll pay.", "Tick the box to agree to the waiver." and "Enter the name of the person signing.". The signature label adds "(parent or guardian)" for under-18s; an adult's signer name is prefilled; "Read all" opens the whole waiver; a privacy line sits under Send. The Registered page has a Due by · For · Amount table and the Kukkiwon steps wording; the printout adds the Dan's requirements fully opened; Register another refills the email and plan (no combined schedule). The results line waits for email. If registration closes while a family is filling in, nothing is sent and the summary stays on screen. **Candidates and payments:** possible duplicates stay out of the cards until resolved; "Late" shows only the overdue amount; rows are ordered by Dan, then school, then last name; tablet and phone layouts as drawn; the mat-side tablet setting hides the cards, money and payment buttons; staff see a gray line once registration has closed. In the candidate detail, Record payment stays available on paid-in-full candidates; Remove uses Star's Withdraw / Delete permanently; there is no history section (the change log keeps everything). In Record payment, "For" is a checklist of unpaid items that fills the Amount with their sum (editable), with an "Owes … due today" line and "Save and record another"; one check covering two items is one line ("Payment 1 + Kukkiwon"). Admin payment actions: Edit needs a reason; Undo is instant with a toast; a refund can't exceed what's been paid; Mark check returned has a note only. Change plan or Kukkiwon after a payment (admins only) shows the "already paid … balance worked out again" line, a Kukkiwon fee added late is due at once, and plans carry a "Current" tag. Add candidate at the desk requires the "Waiver signed on paper" tick, gives a BB-#### reference (unlike Star), opens straight away, and treats due dates already passed as due today. The receipt opens a preview with "Print or save as PDF"; notes, "recorded by", DOB and email never print. Overpaid shows "Paid in full · ⚠ $X overpaid" with "Record refund · $X overpaid" (admins). Paid in full needs the plan and the Kukkiwon fee, and a late Kukkiwon fee is named in the tag. **Next:** database plan and build contract for cycles, registration and payments; grading questions once Master Jeong answers part A. |
| Black Belt testing database: where the requirements panel gets its numbers (decided v0.901) | Database question Q-B1. Jeffrey chose **B: a requirements sheet now, the rubric in spring** (2026-10-19). Until grading is built, each cycle stores **one requirements sheet**: a single structured document holding the parts, subjects, credits, scoring tables and pass marks. It is copied from a starting sheet that migration 0016 loads with the **Fall 2026 numbers**. The family form's "What [Dan] is tested on" panel (v0.919) draws from it, and families see exactly the approved panel. The sheet has **no edit screen**: until grading exists, a number change is a small SQL update that Jeffrey pastes. **In spring, after Master Jeong's rules answers**, the real rubric tables are built once (in the grading migration), the panel switches to them, and the sheet is dropped. Rejected: A (build the rubric tables now, before the rules are confirmed); C (no panel until grading; link to the old `requirements.html`). Jeffrey also considered keeping a family-facing sheet for good beside the rubric, and chose not to. Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-DB-Questions.html` (Q-B1 and its explainer). |
| Black Belt testing: a cycle's prices are set when it is saved (decided v0.902; changes v0.217 and v0.900) | Database question Q-B2 (Jeffrey, 2026-10-19). **Prices live only on each cycle.** **Add cycle** shows the cycle's payment plans (name, number of payments, amount per payment for each Dan), in order, and the **Kukkiwon fee per Dan**. All of these are **filled in from the previous cycle** and can be changed there: amounts edited, a plan added or removed, plans reordered. **Once the cycle is saved, its prices are set** for that testing and can't be changed again, even before anyone registers or pays. Edit cycle shows them read-only with "Prices are set when the cycle is saved." The **next** cycle's Add cycle starts from these prices. The very first cycle starts from the Fall 2026 prices, loaded once by migration 0016. **Rubric and prices loses its payment plans and Kukkiwon fee sections**: it keeps the waiver (v0.213), and later the rubric. This replaces v0.217's "plans and amounts on Rubric and prices, copied into each cycle", screen 6's price sections, and screen 4's "Offer this plan" ticks (a season simply removes a plan in Add cycle). Changing one family's plan (screen 23) is unchanged. Jeffrey was shown the cost: a mistyped price saved by mistake can't be fixed; the cycle would have to be deleted, which is refused once anyone has registered. Rejected: "Copy prices again" from Rubric and prices; a separate price grid editable on each cycle until the first payment; fixable until the first registration; delete and re-add. Screens 4 and 6 are redrawn in `TKD-BlackBelt-Cycles-Mockups.html`. |
| Black Belt testing database: each payment remembers its school and cycle (decided v0.903) | Database question Q-B3. Jeffrey chose **A** (2026-10-19). When a payment is recorded, its row stores the **candidate's school** and the **cycle's name** as they are at that moment. A reversal (undo or edit), refund or returned-check row copies them from the payment it corrects, so one chain of rows never splits between two schools. **Finance's Daily report uses the stored school.** If staff later fix a candidate's school, money already recorded stays with the school it was paid at (closed days never change because of a correction), and only later payments count for the new school. If old money really should move, an admin undoes it and records it again. A renamed cycle keeps its old name on old rows. This matches the note sent to the Finance session. Rejected: B (store the school, read the cycle name fresh); C (read both fresh each time, as Star does; a fix would move counted money and mark closed days "Changed after closing"). Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-DB-Questions.html` (Q-B3). |
| Black Belt testing database: payment records are never deleted (decided v0.904) | Database question Q-B4. Jeffrey chose **A, never** (2026-10-19). Once a candidate has **any** payment record (even one that was undone), **Delete permanently** is grayed out with "[Name] has payment records, so they can't be deleted. Withdraw them instead." Withdraw (hidden under "Removed", restorable, payments kept) is the way out. Likewise a cycle with any payment record can't be deleted (v0.900). Enforced in the database: nothing can delete a payment row, not even a cascade from deleting a candidate or cycle. Every key from `bb_payments` is `on delete restrict`, and a guard refuses every delete with no exception. So Finance's closed days never lose lines. Jeffrey accepted the cost: a mistaken test payment means that candidate can only be withdrawn. Rejected: B (Star's Delete permanently also removes the payments, after a warning); C (delete allowed when the payments add up to $0). Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-DB-Questions.html` (Q-B4). |
| Black Belt testing database: the waiver changes while a family is filling in (decided v0.905) | Database question Q-B5. Jeffrey chose **A** (2026-10-19). The form sends the waiver version the family had on screen. The database **accepts that version if it was the current one at any time in the last 24 hours**, and the signed record stores exactly that version (e.g. "Signed by Jordan Kim (parent) · version 2027-1" even after 2027-2 was saved). An older version (a page left open for days) is refused, and the form shows "Please reload the page". Registrations already sent always keep their version. Jeffrey accepted that for up to a day after a change, someone can still sign the previous text. Rejected: B (refuse and show the new text to read and tick again). Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-DB-Questions.html` (Q-B5). |
| Black Belt testing database: what is removed when a waiver's keeping time ends (decided v0.906) | Database question Q-B6. Jeffrey chose **A** (2026-10-19). On the waiver's deletion date (the later of last testing day + N1 years and 18th birthday + N2 years; both numbers from the insurer, v0.213/v0.900), the dashboard automatically removes the **drawn signature and the signer's name**. The signer's name is also hidden in the change log, so it is really gone. It **keeps that a waiver was signed, when, and which version**. The candidate detail then reads e.g. "Signed on Mar 10, 2027 · version 2027-1 · signature and signer's name deleted on Mar 4, 2033 after the keeping time". Payments, Dan and results are never removed by this. Rejected: B (remove the whole waiver record); C (signature only, as Star; the parent's name would stay forever). Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-DB-Questions.html` (Q-B6). |
| Black Belt testing database: how long date of birth and email are kept (decided v0.907) | Database question Q-B7. Jeffrey chose **C** (2026-10-19). A Black Belt candidate's **date of birth and email are removed on the same day as their waiver's signature and signer's name** (v0.906): the later of last testing day + N1 years and 18th birthday + N2 years. They are **also hidden in the change log** (shown as "changed"), so the old values aren't kept forever. **Names, Dan, results and payments are kept.** The candidate detail then shows "removed [date]" for date of birth and email. One date per candidate, and no extra setting. This differs from Color Belt and Star, which keep them (those sections are not changed). Rejected: A (keep forever, like Color Belt and Star); B (remove a set number of years after the cycle, a third setting). Drawn in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-DB-Questions.html` (Q-B7). |
| Black Belt testing database: the smaller defaults (decided v0.908) | Jeffrey OK'd all 19 smaller defaults in `docs/Testing/Black Belt Testing/TKD-BlackBelt-DB-Plan.md` §8 (2026-10-19), shown in plain words in `TKD-BlackBelt-DB-Questions.html` (defaults sheet). In short: a payment pays its ticked "For" items first, then the earliest unpaid (S1). Four kinds of money lines: payment, refund (never more than paid), undo, returned check. No new payment on a withdrawn candidate; no edit while a refund is active (S2). $5,000 per payment; prices in whole dollars, $1–$5,000 (S3). A late family's past due dates count from the day they registered; a late Kukkiwon fee is due that day (S4). Admins only change a Dan after a payment (S8). Date of birth and email are required at the desk too (S5). Duplicates match on same cycle, name and date of birth (S7). The first Add cycle starts from the Fall 2026 schedule, which is not a cycle itself (S9). No student link yet (S10). References run from BB-5001 on one counter (S14). Delete cycle is refused once there is any payment or online-signed waiver (S15). Date of birth, email and signer names are masked in the change log (S11). Nothing is deleted until the insurer's numbers are entered; then 3–20 years, with a confirm, logged (S12). Deletions run in the background on staff reads (S13). Only admins open a signature (S18). Black Belt has its own tables, with each cycle's prices as checked rows locked when saved (S6). Finance reads only the payment lines, the stored school and cycle, and the candidate's name (S17). It reuses Color Belt's date and name helpers (S19). Receipt prints and QR downloads aren't logged (S20). **Next:** the build contract `CONTRACT-0016.md` (migration 0016, to reserve with the Main session before SQL), then a security review, then Jeffrey's OK. |

Open items: **OI-7** no hard daily cap on online registrations per cycle (the 20-an-hour-per-connection limit and the bot check stay; the daily count is logged and the database size is checked at CP3 / CP5). **OI-8** Delete permanently (only for a candidate with no payments, meant for fake and test registrations) also deletes their online-signed waiver; real families are withdrawn, which keeps everything. OI-1–OI-6 use the contract's defaults (opening registration waits for the real waiver text; a "Waiver signed on paper" tick for the parent's-signature warning, shown at CP2; school phones as constants; a refund copies the school and cycle from the latest live payment; Turnstile action made a parameter; two keep-years fields, shown at CP2). Nothing is applied to Supabase without Jeffrey's OK. |

This can't be taken back; if it was right, record it again." → **Undo payment** / Cancel; then the toast "Payment 2 undone · $290 · Alex Kim" **without Undo**. Recording a payment keeps its 10-second Undo in the toast (staff and admins), and Edit payment keeps its Undo. Rejected: B, undo at once and let the toast's Undo record the payment again (three lines, and the payment's date moves to today on the Daily report). |

Rejected: A (Back leaves the whole event). Add candidates on the Candidates tab."); "Couldn’t load the grades. The grid has been reloaded."); the phone picker's "1st Dan · 8 candidates" / "Pick who to grade"; Choose subjects' "Tick at least one subject.", stale, no-longer-needed, admin-only and connection messages; "Couldn’t load the private testing. Check the connection, then try again."; and the three banners when the private page closes (moved back to the testing day / withdrawn / deleted on another device). **Build notes (as built, seen in preview):** phones under 640px wide grade one candidate at a time; 640–1023px (the upright tablet) keeps the full grid with cells at least 32px (the grid scrolls inside its own box if needed; v0.512 exception); 1024px and up on touch uses 44px rows, and every other touch control is 44px; a normal row's Total shows "—" until it is complete (a make-up shows "so far"); the gray "Changed on another device" note sits under the candidate's name on the grid; an automatic retry waits for a fresh read before it resends; the top-bar school switch asks the leave question while anything is Not saved. **Known limitation (security review):** the private page reads the candidate with `star_candidate_detail`, so date of birth, email and payments reach the browser (never shown or kept); a grading-only read needs a migration (status file). |
